# **II. Software Requirement Specification** {#ii.-software-requirement-specification}

## **1\. Overall Requirements** {#1.-overall-requirements}

### **1.1 Context Diagram** {#1.1-context-diagram}

PhucNguyen Resort & Tour Hub is a web-based integrated resort management system that combines hotel booking, restaurant services, tour booking, billing, and operational management into a single platform. The system enables guests to conveniently access resort services while helping staff and managers efficiently manage daily operations, payments, customer information, and business performance through centralized dashboards and reports.

![][image2]  
**Figure I.1: Context Diagram**  
[Context Diagram](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1XRMbIP6WZve9AEwi9-O9u4jakGDQtOvt/view)

##   [Draw.io](https://app.diagrams.net/#G1Jfncagr45hIDjmARLEsw_nMWVPOls5Hm#%7B%22pageId%22%3A%221h3P12EfJgsQ4Wb-FNXP%22%7D)

The system is developed using the following technologies:  
• Programming Language: C\#  
• Framework: ASP.NET Core MVC .net8  
• Database:SQL Server

From context diagram to use case modeling

### **1.2 User Requirements** {#1.2-user-requirements}

#### ***1.2.1 Actors*** {#1.2.1-actors}

| \# | Actor | Description |
| ----- | :---- | :---- |
| 1 | Guest | An unauthenticated user who accesses the system without logging in. Guests can browse resort information, search available rooms, view restaurant menus, explore tour packages, read customer reviews, and register for an account. |
| 2 | User | The user represents all external entities, specifically human beings. Users can log in, manage their personal profile, update personal information, and access authenticated system features. |
| 3 | Customer | A registered and authenticated user who actively uses resort services. Customers can book rooms, make deposit payments, reserve restaurant tables, order food, book tours, view invoices, submit reviews, and request personal data deletion. |
| 4 | Admin | The system administrator with the highest level of authority. Responsible for managing the entire system, user accounts, role assignments, account suspension/reactivation, data backup and recovery, and monitoring system activities. |
| 5 | Business Manager | The resort operations manager responsible for managing business-related data such as room categories, restaurant menus, and tour packages. The manager can monitor revenue, occupancy rates, financial reports, customer reviews, and overall business performance. |
| 6 | Receptionist | Front desk staff responsible for managing reservations, verifying guest information, performing check-in and check-out procedures, assigning rooms, updating room statuses, processing final payments, and generating consolidated invoices. |
| 7 | Housekeeping Staff | Staff responsible for maintaining room cleanliness. They can view cleaning tasks, update room cleaning statuses, mark rooms as cleaned, and report maintenance issues when necessary. |
| 8 | Maintenance Staff | Technical staff responsible for inspecting, repairing, and maintaining resort facilities and equipment. They manage maintenance requests, perform repairs, and update maintenance task statuses. |
| 9 | F\&B Staff | Restaurant and room service staff responsible for handling food orders, managing table reservations, updating order statuses, delivering food, and charging food expenses to guest room accounts. |
| 10 | Kitchen Staff | Kitchen personnel responsible for receiving Kitchen Order Tickets (KOTs), preparing food, updating food preparation statuses, and marking orders as ready for service. |

 

#### ***1.2.2 Use Cases Description (UC)*** {#1.2.2-use-cases-description-(uc)}

| ID | Use Case | Actor(s) | Use Case Description |
| ----- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| UC01 | Login | Guest | As a guest, I want to log into the system so that I can access features according to my role. |
| UC02 | View Homepage | Guest | As a guest, I want to view the homepage so that I can access resort information and available services. |
| UC03 | View Rooms | Guest | As a guest, I want to browse available rooms so that I can find suitable accommodation. |
| UC04 | Search Room | Guest | As a guest, I want to search rooms based on my preferences so that I can find suitable rooms quickly. |
| UC05 | Filter Room | Guest | As a guest, I want to filter rooms by category, price, and capacity so that I can narrow down my options. |
| UC06 | View Room Details | Guest | As a guest, I want to view room details so that I can make an informed booking decision. |
| UC07 | View Restaurant Table | Guest | As a guest, I want to view restaurant tables so that I can reserve a table if needed. |
|  UC08 |  View Table Details |  Guest |  As a guest, I want to view table details so that I can select a suitable table. |
| UC09 | View Restaurant Menu | Guest | As a guest, I want to browse the restaurant menu so that I can explore available dishes. |
| UC10 | View Food Details | Guest | As a guest, I want to view food details so that I can know ingredients, prices, and descriptions. |
| UC11 | View Tours | Guest | As a guest, I want to browse available tours so that I can plan activities during my stay. |
| UC12 | View Tour Details | Guest | As a guest, I want to view detailed tour information so that I can decide whether to book it. |
| UC13 | View Resort Information | Guest | As a guest, I want to view resort information so that I can learn about facilities and services. |
| UC14 | View Reviews | Guest | As a guest, I want to view customer reviews so that I can evaluate service quality. |
| UC15 | View Profile | Registered User | As a registered user, I want to view my profile information so that I can verify my account details. |
| UC16 | Update Profile | Registered User | As a registered user, I want to update my profile information so that my account information remains accurate. |
| UC17 | Update CCCD/Passport | Registered User | As a registered user, I want to upload or update my identification documents for verification purposes. |
| UC18 | Change Password | Registered User | As a registered user, I want to change my password so that I can secure my account. |
| UC19 | Forgot Password | Registered User | As a registered user, I want to reset my password when I forget it so that I can regain access to my account. |
| UC20 | Logout | Registered User | As a registered user, I want to log out of the system so that my account remains secure. |
| UC21 | Register Account | Customer | As a customer, I want to create an account so that I can use resort services. |
| UC22 | Book Room | Customer | As a customer, I want to book a room so that I can stay at the resort. |
| UC23 | Payment | Customer | As a customer, I want to pay deposits and service fees online so that my bookings can be confirmed. |
| UC24 | Reserve Restaurant Table | Customer | As a customer, I want to reserve a restaurant table so that I can dine at my preferred time. |
| UC25 | Order Food To Room | Customer | As a customer, I want to order food to my room so that I can enjoy room service. |
| UC26 | Book Tour | Customer | As a customer, I want to book a tour so that I can participate in resort activities. |
| UC27 | View Personal Invoice | Customer | As a customer, I want to view my invoices so that I can track my expenses. |
| UC28 | View Booking History | Customer | As a customer, I want to view my booking history so that I can review past reservations. |
| UC29 | Submit Reviews | Customer | As a customer, I want to submit reviews so that I can share my experience. |
| UC30 | Receive Notifications | Customer | As a customer, I want to receive notifications so that I can stay updated about bookings and services. |
| UC31 | View Dashboard | Admin | As an administrator, I want to view the system dashboard so that I can monitor system activities. |
| UC32 | View Account | Admin | As an administrator, I want to manage user accounts so that I can control system access. |
| UC33 | Create Account | Admin | As an administrator, I want to create staff accounts so that employees can access the system. |
| UC34 | Search Account | Admin | As an administrator, I want to search user accounts so that I can manage them efficiently. |
| UC35 | Sort Account | Admin | As an administrator, I want to sort accounts so that I can find information quickly. |
| UC36 | Block Account | Admin | As an administrator, I want to block accounts that violate system policies. |
| UC37 | Backup Database | Admin | As an administrator, I want to back up the database so that system data is protected. |
| UC38 | Restore Database | Admin | As an administrator, I want to restore the database so that data can be recovered when needed. |
| UC39 | View Business Dashboard | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to view the business dashboard so that I can monitor overall resort performance, including revenue statistics, occupancy rates, and operational indicators.  |
| UC40 | Manage Rooms  | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to manage room information so that room categories, pricing, availability, and operational data remain accurate and up to date.  |
| UC41 | Manage Tours  | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to manage tour packages so that tour information, schedules, pricing, and availability can be maintained effectively.  |
| UC42 | Manage Menu  | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to manage restaurant menu items so that food and beverage offerings remain accurate and updated.  |
| UC43 | Manage Tables  | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to manage restaurant tables so that seating arrangements and table availability can be organized efficiently.  |
| UC44 | View Financial Report | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to view financial reports so that I can evaluate business performance, analyze revenue sources, and monitor financial results.  |
| UC45 | Export PDF/Excel Report | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to export financial reports to PDF or Excel format so that business information can be archived, shared, and analyzed externally.  |
|  UC46 |  Manage Customer Reviews  |  Business Manager |  As a business manager, I want to manage customer reviews so that guest feedback can be monitored and inappropriate content can be moderated.  |
| UC47 | View Customer Reviews | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to view customer reviews so that I can understand customer satisfaction and identify areas for service improvement.  |
| UC48 | Approve/Hide Review  | Business Manager | As a business manager, I want to approve or hide customer reviews so that only appropriate and relevant feedback is displayed on the public portal.  |
| UC49 | Manage Room Bookings | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to manage room bookings so that I can search, view, verify, and handle reservation information properly.  |
| UC50 | View Daily Arrival/Departure List | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to view daily arrivals and departures so that I can prepare front-desk operations for check-in and check-out.  |
| UC51 | Perform Customer Check-in  | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to check guests in so that they can begin their stay after their booking and identity information are verified.  |
| UC52 | Submit Stay Registration | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to submit guest stay registration information so that the resort can comply with temporary residence regulations.  |
| UC53 | Perform Customer Check-out | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to check guests out so that their stay can be completed after all charges are reviewed and settled.  |
| UC54 | Generate Consolidated Invoice | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to generate a consolidated invoice including room charges, food orders, and tour bookings so that the guest can review the total amount payable.  |
| UC55 | Process Final Payment | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to process the final payment so that the consolidated invoice can be marked as paid and the check-out process can be completed.  |
| UC56 | Update Room Operational Status | Receptionist | As a receptionist, I want to update room operational statuses so that room availability and operational conditions remain accurate.  |
| UC57 | Create Maintenance Request  | Receptionist  | As a receptionist, I want to create maintenance requests when room issues are reported so that maintenance staff can inspect and resolve the problems.  |
| UC58 | Export Temporary Residence Report  | Receptionist  | As a receptionist, I want to export temporary residence reports so that guest stay information can be submitted to the required local authority when needed.  |
| UC59 | View Dashboard | Housekeeping Staff | As housekeeping staff, I want to view my dashboard so that I can monitor assigned tasks. |
| UC60 | View Room Cleaning Tasks | Housekeeping Staff | As housekeeping staff, I want to view cleaning tasks so that I know which rooms require cleaning. |
| UC61 | Update Room Cleaning Status | Housekeeping Staff | As housekeeping staff, I want to update cleaning status so that room availability is accurate. |
| UC62 | View Cleaning History | Housekeeping Staff | As housekeeping staff, I want to view cleaning history so that I can track completed tasks. |
| UC63 | Report Room Issues | Housekeeping Staff | As housekeeping staff, I want to report room issues so that maintenance staff can resolve them. |
| UC64 | View Dashboard | F\&B Staff | As an F\&B Staff, I want to view the operational dashboard so that I can monitor food orders, table reservations, and restaurant activities in real time. |
| UC65 | View Food Orders | F\&B Staff | As an F\&B Staff, I want to view all food orders so that I can track customer requests and manage the order workflow efficiently. |
| UC66 | Update Food Order Status | F\&B Staff | As an F\&B Staff, I want to update the status of food orders (Pending → Cooking → Delivered) so that customers and other staff can track order progress. |
| UC67 | View Table Reservations | F\&B Staff | As an F\&B Staff, I want to view table reservations so that I can prepare tables and manage restaurant seating arrangements. |
| UC68 | Update Table Status | F\&B Staff | As an F\&B Staff, I want to update table status (Available, Reserved, Occupied) so that reservation information remains accurate. |
| UC69 | Charge Food Bill To Room | F\&B Staff | As an F\&B Staff, I want to charge a customer's food bill directly to their room so that the amount can be included in the final consolidated invoice. |
| UC70 | View Dashboard | Kitchen Staff | As a Kitchen Staff member, I want to view the kitchen dashboard so that I can monitor incoming orders and kitchen workload. |
| UC71 | View Kitchen Order Tickets (KOT) | Kitchen Staff | As a Kitchen Staff member, I want to view all Kitchen Order Tickets (KOT) so that I can prepare food according to customer requests. |
| UC72 | View KOT Details | Kitchen Staff | As a Kitchen Staff member, I want to view detailed information of a KOT so that I can prepare the correct dishes and quantities. |
| UC73 | Update KOT Status | Kitchen Staff | As a Kitchen Staff member, I want to update the status of a KOT (Pending → Preparing → Ready to Serve) so that F\&B Staff can know when orders are ready for delivery. |

#### ***1.3.3 Non-UI Functions*** {#1.3.3-non-ui-functions}

| \# | Feature | System Function | Description |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| 1 | Authentication | Password Hashing | Hash passwords before storing. |
| 2 | Authentication | Email Verification Token | Generate verification tokens. |
| 3 | Authentication | Password Reset Token | Generate reset-password tokens. |
| 4 | Authorization | Role-Based Access Control | Authorize users by role. |
| 5 | Profile | Encrypt ID/Passport Data | Protect sensitive identity information. |
| 6 | Profile | Data Deletion Request | Process right-to-erasure requests. |
| 7 | Booking | Check Room Availability | Validate room availability. |
| 8 | Booking | Prevent Double Booking | Prevent overlapping reservations. |
| 9 | Booking | Booking Code Generation | Generate unique booking code. |
| 10 | Booking | Deposit Calculation | Calculate deposit amount. |
| 11 | Payment | Payment Gateway Integration | Connect VNPay/Stripe. |
| 12 | Payment | Payment Callback Handler | Handle payment response. |
| 13 | Notification | Booking Confirmation | Send email/SMS confirmation. |
| 14 | Notification | Digital Invoice Delivery | Send invoices electronically. |
| 15 | Front Desk | Assign Physical Room | Assign room during check-in. |
| 16 | Front Desk | Temporary Residence Validation | Validate residence registration data. |
| 17 | Room Status | State Transition Management | Manage room lifecycle status. |
| 18 | Housekeeping | Generate Cleaning Tasks | Create cleaning jobs automatically. |
| 19 | Maintenance | Maintenance Request Creation | Create maintenance tickets. |
| 20 | Maintenance | Maintenance Status Tracking | Track maintenance progress. |
| 21 | Restaurant | Table Availability Check | Check table availability. |
| 22 | Restaurant | Prevent Table Double Booking | Prevent table overlaps. |
| 23 | Restaurant | Food Order Creation | Create food orders. |
| 24 | Kitchen | Generate KOT | Generate Kitchen Order Tickets. |
| 25 | Kitchen | KOT Status Synchronization | Update KOT workflow status. |
| 26 | F\&B | Charge To Room | Push restaurant charges to room folio. |
| 27 | Tour | Tour Schedule Validation | Validate tour capacity and schedule. |
| 28 | Tour | Weather API Integration | Retrieve weather information. |
| 29 | Tour | Map API Integration | Display map/location information. |
| 30 | Feedback | Review Moderation Logic | Control review visibility. |
| 31 | Billing | Guest Folio Creation | Create folio per stay. |
| 32 | Billing | Add Room Charges | Post room charges. |
| 33 | Billing | Add Food Charges | Post F\&B charges. |
| 34 | Billing | Add Tour Charges | Post tour charges. |
| 35 | Billing | Outstanding Charge Validation | Prevent checkout with unpaid items. |
| 36 | Billing | Consolidated Invoice Generation | Generate final invoice. |
| 37 | Billing | Final Payment Processing | Process checkout payment. |
| 38 | Refund | Refund Calculation | Apply cancellation policy. |
| 39 | Dashboard | Occupancy Rate Calculation | Calculate occupancy KPIs. |
| 40 | Dashboard | Revenue Breakdown Calculation | Calculate revenue by source. |
| 41 | Report | Excel/PDF Export | Export reports. |
| 42 | Admin | Staff Account Creation | Create employee accounts. |

| 45 | Admin | Database Restore | Restore database. |
| 46 | Audit | System Activity Logging | Record user activities. |
| 47 | Audit | Error Logging | Record application errors. |
| 48 | Night Audit | Daily Closing Process | Finalize daily business data. |
| 49 | Notification | Tour Ticket QR Generation | Generate QR tickets. |
| 50 | Security | Session Management | Manage login sessions. |

## **2\. Use Case Specifications** {#2.-use-case-specifications}

### **2.1 Auth, Profiles & Master Data**  {#2.1-auth,-profiles-&-master-data}

#### ***2.1.1 UC21 – Register Account***  {#2.1.1-uc21-–-register-account}

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC21 |
| Use Case Name | Register Account |
| Feature / Module | Auth, Profiles & Master Data |
| Primary Actor | Guest |
| Secondary Actors | Email Verification Service |
| Description | As a guest, I want to create an account so that I can use resort services such as booking rooms, reserving tables, ordering food, booking tours, and viewing invoices. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Guest is not logged in. 2\. Guest accesses the Register page. 3\. The email entered by the guest has not been used by another account. 4\. The system is connected to the email verification service. |
| Postconditions | 1\. A new user account is created with Customer role. 2\. Account status is set to Pending Verification or Inactive until email verification is completed. 3\. A verification email is sent to the registered email address. 4\. Registration activity is recorded in the system log. |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Guest clicks Register from the page header or registration entry point. 2\. System displays the Register Account screen. 3\. Guest enters registration information, including full name, email, phone number, password, and confirm password. 4\. Guest clicks the Register button. 5\. System validates required fields. 6\. System validates email format. 7\. System validates phone number format. 8\. System validates password strength. 9\. System checks whether password and confirm password match. 10\. System checks whether the email already exists in the database. 11\. System hashes the password before storing it. 12\. System creates a new user account with role Customer. 13\. System creates the corresponding user profile record. 14\. System generates an email verification token. 15\. System sends a verification email to the guest. 16\. System displays a successful registration message and asks the guest to verify email before logging in. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow  A1 – Email already exists | At step 10, if the email already exists: 1\. System does not create a new account. 2\. System displays message: “This email is already registered.” 3\. Guest may return to the login page or use forgot password. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow  A2 – Invalid input data | At steps 5–9, if input data is invalid: 1\. System highlights invalid fields. 2\. System displays the corresponding validation message. 3\. Guest corrects the information. 4\. Flow returns to step 4 of the normal flow. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow  A3 – Email verification service unavailable | At step 15, if the email service is unavailable: 1\. System creates the account with Pending Verification status. 2\. System records the failed email sending event. 3\. System displays message: “Account created, but verification email could not be sent. Please request email verification again later.” |
| Exception Flow  E1 \- System/DB error | If a system or database error occurs during account creation: 1\. System rolls back account creation transaction. 2\. System displays a general error message. 3\. System records the error in the system log. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC21-01: Email must be unique in the system. BR-UC21-02: Password must satisfy the system password policy. BR-UC21-03: Password must be stored using hashing, not plain text. BR-UC21-04: New registered account must not access authenticated customer features until email verification is completed. BR-UC21-05: Default role for self-registration is Customer. |
| Related Use Cases | UC01 Login UC19 Forgot Password UC20 Logout UC16 Update Profile UC17 Update CCCD/Passport |
| Related Components | **Controllers**: AuthController, RegisterController **Services:** AuthService, UserService, EmailVerificationService, PasswordHashingService **Repositories / DAO:** UserRepository, UserProfileRepository, RoleRepository, VerificationTokenRepository **Entities / Tables:** Users, UserProfiles, Roles, VerificationTokens, ActivityLogs |
| Input Data | Full name, email, phone number, password, confirm password |
| Output Data | Registration result message, verification email, pending customer account |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | Medium |

#### 

#### ***2.1.2 UC01 – Login*** 

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC01 |
| Use Case Name | Login |
| Feature / Module | Auth, Profiles & Master Data |
| Primary Actor | Registered User |
| Secondary Actors | None |
| Description | As a registered user, I want to log into the system so that I can access system features according to my assigned role. |
| Preconditions | 1\. User account already exists. 2\. User account is active. 3\. User is not currently logged in. |
| Postconditions | 1\. User logs in successfully. 2\. Authenticated session is created. 3\. User role and permissions are loaded. 4\. Login activity is recorded. |
| Input Data | Email, password |
| Output Data | Login result message, authenticated session, user profile, user role, access permissions |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. User opens the Login page. 2\. System displays the Login form. 3\. User enters email and password. 4\. User submits the login request. 5\. System authenticates the user information. 6\. System creates an authenticated session. 7\. System redirects the user to the appropriate page based on role. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Invalid login information | At step 5, if email or password is incorrect: 1\. System rejects the login request. 2\. System displays an invalid login message. 3\. User may enter login information again. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Account inactive or blocked | At step 5, if the account is inactive or blocked: 1\. System denies access. 2\. System displays an account status warning message. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Email not verified | At step 5, if the email has not been verified: 1\. System denies access. 2\. System asks the user to verify email before logging in. |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error | If system error occurs during login: 1\. System does not create a session. 2\. System displays a general error message. 3\. System records the error. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC01-01: Email and password are required. BR-UC01-02: Password must be verified against stored hashed password. BR-UC01-03: Only active accounts can access the system. BR-UC01-04: User permissions are determined by assigned role. BR-UC01-05: Failed login attempts should be tracked for security. |
| Related Use Cases | UC21 Register Account UC19 Forgot Password UC20 Logout |
| Related Components | Controllers: AuthController, LoginController Services: AuthService, UserService, RoleService, PasswordHashingService Repositories / DAO: UserRepository, RoleRepository, ActivityLogRepository Entities / Tables: Users, Roles, ActivityLogs |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | Very High |
| Entities Affected | Users, Roles, ActivityLogs |

#### 

#### ***2.1.3 UC17 – Update CCCD/Passport***  {#2.1.3-uc17-–-update-cccd/passport}

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC17 |
| Use Case Name | Update CCCD/Passport |
| Feature / Module | Auth, Profiles & Master Data |
| Primary Actor | Registered User |
| Secondary Actors | File Storage Service |
| Description | As a registered user, I want to upload or update my CCCD/Passport information so that my identity can be verified for booking, check-in, and stay registration processes. |
| Preconditions | 1\. User has logged into the system. 2\. User profile already exists. 3\. User accesses the Update CCCD/Passport page. 4\. User agrees to provide identity information for verification purposes. |
| Postconditions | 1\. CCCD/Passport information is saved or updated. 2\. Uploaded document file is stored securely. 3\. Identity verification status is updated. 4\. Update activity is recorded. |
| Input Data | Document type, CCCD/Passport number, full name on document, nationality, date of birth, issue date, expiry date, uploaded document image/file, consent confirmation |
| Output Data | Updated identity document record, updated verification status, success/error message |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. User opens the Profile page. 2\. User selects Update CCCD/Passport. 3\. System displays the identity document form. 4\. User enters document information and uploads document file. 5\. User confirms consent and submits the form. 6\. System validates the submitted information and file. 7\. System saves or updates the identity document information securely. 8\. System displays the update result. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Invalid document information | At step 6, if document information is invalid: 1\. System rejects the update. 2\. System displays validation messages. 3\. User corrects the information and submits again. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Invalid file upload | At step 6, if uploaded file is invalid: 1\. System rejects the file. 2\. System displays an invalid file message. 3\. User uploads another file. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Duplicate document number | At step 6, if the document number is already used by another account: 1\. System does not save the update. 2\. System displays duplicate document message. |
| Exception Flow E1 – File storage error | If the document file cannot be stored: 1\. System cancels the update. 2\. System displays a general error message. 3\. System records the error. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC17-01: User must provide consent before identity document information is stored. BR-UC17-02: CCCD/Passport number must be unique among active accounts. BR-UC17-03: Only allowed file types can be uploaded. BR-UC17-04: Sensitive identity data must be stored securely. BR-UC17-05: Updating identity information changes verification status to pending until verified. |
| Related Use Cases | UC16 Update Profile UC51 Perform Customer Check-in UC52 Submit Stay Registration |
| Related Components | Controllers: ProfileController, IdentityDocumentController Services: UserProfileService, IdentityDocumentService, FileStorageService, EncryptionService Repositories / DAO: UserProfileRepository, IdentityDocumentRepository, UserRepository, ActivityLogRepository Entities / Tables: Users, UserProfiles, IdentityDocuments, ActivityLogs |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | Low |
| Entities Affected | Users, UserProfiles, IdentityDocuments, ActivityLogs |

#### ***2.1.4 UC33 – Create Staff Account*** 

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC33 |
| Use Case Name | Create Staff Account |
| Feature / Module | Auth, Profiles & Master Data |
| Primary Actor | Admin |
| Secondary Actors | Email Service |
| Description | As an administrator, I want to create staff accounts so that resort employees can access the system according to their assigned roles and responsibilities. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Admin has logged into the system. 2\. Admin has permission to manage staff accounts. 3\. Staff role already exists in the system. |
| Postconditions | 1\. New staff account is created. 2\. Staff role is assigned. 3\. Staff account status is set according to system policy. 4\. Activation or account information email is sent to the staff member. 5\. Account creation activity is recorded. |
| Input Data | Full name, email, phone number, staff role, initial status, optional initial password |
| Output Data | Created staff account, assigned role, activation email, success/error message |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Admin opens Staff Account Management page. 2\. System displays staff account management screen. 3\. Admin selects Create Staff Account. 4\. Admin enters staff information and selects staff role. 5\. Admin submits the account creation request. 6\. System validates staff information and role. 7\. System creates the staff account and assigns the selected role. 8\. System sends activation or account information email. 9\. System displays account creation result. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Email already exists | At step 6, if the email already exists: 1\. System rejects account creation. 2\. System displays duplicate email message. 3\. Admin may correct the email and submit again. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Invalid role selected | At step 6, if the selected role is invalid or inactive: 1\. System rejects account creation. 2\. System displays invalid role message. 3\. Admin selects another role. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Email service unavailable | At step 8, if email sending fails: 1\. System keeps the account created with pending activation status. 2\. System records email sending failure. 3\. System displays a warning message. |
| Exception Flow E1 – Database error | If a database error occurs during account creation: 1\. System rolls back account creation. 2\. System displays a general error message. 3\. System records the error. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC33-01: Only Admin can create staff accounts. BR-UC33-02: Staff email must be unique. BR-UC33-03: Staff account must be assigned a valid role. BR-UC33-04: Password must be hashed before storage. BR-UC33-05: Staff may be required to change password on first login. |
| Related Use Cases | UC01 Login UC32 View Account UC34 Search Account UC36 Block Account |
| Related Components | Controllers: AdminController, StaffAccountController Services: UserService, StaffAccountService, RoleService, EmailService, PasswordHashingService Repositories / DAO: UserRepository, RoleRepository, UserProfileRepository, ActivityLogRepository Entities / Tables: Users, Roles, UserProfiles, ActivityLogs |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | Medium |
| Entities Affected | Users, Roles, UserProfiles, ActivityLogs |

#### ***2.1.5 UC40 – Manage Rooms***

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC40 |
| Use Case Name | Manage Rooms |
| Feature / Module | Auth, Profiles & Master Data |
| Primary Actor | Business Manager |
| Secondary Actors | File Storage Service |
| Description | As a business manager, I want to manage room master data so that room information, room categories, pricing, capacity, images, and availability status remain accurate for room reservation and operational management. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Business Manager is logged into the system. 2\. Business Manager has permission to manage room data. 3\. Room category information already exists in the system. |
| Postconditions | 1\. Room information is created, updated, enabled, disabled, or viewed successfully. 2\. Room catalog is updated and available for related modules. 3\. Room management activity is recorded in the system log. |
| Input Data | Room number, room category, room description, room capacity, price per night, room images, room status |
| Output Data | Updated room record, updated room catalog, success/error message |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Business Manager opens Room Management. 2\. System displays room information. 3\. Business Manager selects an operation (Create, View, Update, Enable, Disable) .4. Business Manager enters or updates room information. 5\. Business Manager submits the changes. 6\. System validates the room information. 7\. System saves the changes. 8\. System displays the operation result. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Invalid room information | At step 6, if room information is invalid: 1\. System rejects the request. 2\. System displays validation messages. 3\. Business Manager updates the information and submits again. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Duplicate room number | At step 6, if room number already exists: 1\. System rejects the request. 2\. System displays duplicate room number message. 3\. Business Manager provides another room number. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Room linked to active bookings | If Business Manager attempts to disable or remove a room with active or future bookings: 1\. System checks related bookings. 2\. System prevents the operation according to business policy. 3\. System displays warning message. |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error | If a system or database error occurs: 1\. System cancels the operation. 2\. System displays a general error message. 3\. System records the error. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC40-01: Room number must be unique. BR-UC40-02: Room capacity must be greater than zero. BR-UC40-03: Room price must be greater than zero. BR-UC40-04: Rooms with historical booking records must not be physically deleted. BR-UC40-05: Only active rooms can be displayed in room search and booking functions. |
| Related Use Cases | UC04 Search Available RoomsUC22 Book RoomUC51 Perform Customer Check-inUC56 Update Room Operational Status |
| Related Components | Controllers: RoomController, RoomManagementController Services: RoomService, RoomCategoryService, FileStorageService Repositories / DAO: RoomRepository, RoomCategoryRepository, BookingRepository, ActivityLogRepository Entities / Tables: Rooms, RoomCategories, Bookings, ActivityLogs |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | Medium |
| Entities Affected | Rooms, RoomCategories, Bookings, ActivityLogs |

#### ***2.1.6 UC41 – Manage Tours*** 

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC41 |
| Use Case Name | Manage Tours |
| Feature / Module | Auth, Profiles & Master Data |
| Primary Actor | Business Manager |
| Secondary Actors | File Storage Service |
| Description | As a business manager, I want to manage tour master data so that tour information, schedules, capacities, pricing, and availability remain accurate for customers. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Business Manager is logged into the system.2. Business Manager has permission to manage tour data. |
| Postconditions | 1\. Tour information is created, updated, enabled, disabled, or viewed successfully. 2\. Tour catalog is updated. |
| Input Data | Tour name, description, itinerary, duration, price, capacity, schedule, image, status |
| Output Data | Updated tour record, updated tour catalog, success/error message |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | Similar to UC40 – Manage Rooms. The managed entity is **Tour** instead of **Room**. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow | Similar to UC40 – Manage Rooms. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC41-01: Tour price must be greater than zero. BR-UC41-02: Tour capacity must be greater than zero. BR-UC41-03: Tours with active bookings cannot be deleted. BR-UC41-04: Only active tours can be booked by customers. |
| Related Use Cases | UC12 View Tour Details UC26 Book Tour UC74 View Tour Manifest |
| Related Components | Controllers: TourController, TourManagementController Services: TourService, TourScheduleService Repositories / DAO: TourRepository, TourScheduleRepository, TourBookingRepository Entities / Tables: Tours, TourSchedules, TourBookings |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | Medium |
| Entities Affected | Tours, TourSchedules, TourBookings |

### **2.2 Room Reservation & Front Desk**  {#2.2-room-reservation-&-front-desk}

#### ***2.2.1 UC04 – Search Available Rooms*** 

| Field                                                             | Specification                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Use Case ID                                                       | UC04                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Use Case Name                                                     | Search Available Rooms                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Feature / Module                                                  | Room Reservation & Front Desk                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Primary Actor                                                     | Guest / Customer                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Secondary Actors                                                  | None                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Description                                                       | As a guest or customer, I want to search available room categories by date range, guest count, and number of rooms so that I can find suitable accommodation for my stay.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Preconditions                                                     | 1\. User accesses the Room Search page (HomeController.SearchRooms). 2\. Room and RoomCategory data already exist in the system.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Postconditions                                                    | 1\. Available room category list is displayed with availability count per category. 2\. User can view room category details or proceed to booking by selecting quantity per category.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Input Data                                                        | Check-in date, check-out date, number of adults, number of children, number of rooms, optional preselected category ID and quantity                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Output Data                                                       | List of available RoomCategories (with images, price per night, max adults, max children, description), available room count per category                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Normal Sequence / Flow                                            | 1\. User opens the Room Search page (/Home/SearchRooms). 2\. System displays the search form with date picker, adults count, children count, and room count fields. 3\. User enters search criteria and submits. 4\. System parses dates (supports formats: dd/MM/yyyy, yyyy-MM-dd, etc.) and validates: check-in ≥ today, check-out > check-in. 5\. System calls HomeService.SearchAvailableCategoriesAsync to query RoomCategories that have enough non-maintenance rooms not overlapping with existing non-cancelled/non-checked-out bookings. 6\. System displays matching room categories with pricing, capacity, images, and availability. 7\. User selects room quantities per category and clicks "Book Now", which redirects to BookingsController.Create. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – No room available                | At step 5, if no category has available rooms: 1\. System displays the category list with zero availability. 2\. User may adjust dates, guest count, or room count and search again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Invalid search criteria          | At step 4, if check-in/check-out dates are invalid or check-out ≤ check-in: 1\. System auto-corrects: sets check-out = check-in + 1 day; sets check-in = today if in past. 2\. Search proceeds with corrected dates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – No filter applied                | If user accesses SearchRooms without date parameters: 1\. System displays all active room categories without availability filtering (isFiltered = false).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A4 – Check room availability via AJAX | User clicks "Check Availability" on a specific category detail page: 1\. System calls HomeController.CheckRoomAvailability (JSON API). 2\. Returns available room count for the category in the given date range.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error                                  | If system error occurs: 1\. System cannot retrieve room availability. 2\. System displays a general error message.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Business Rules                                                    | BR-UC04-01: Check-in date must be today or later. BR-UC04-02: Check-out date must be after check-in date. BR-UC04-03: Rooms with overlapping confirmed/reserved/checked-in bookings (BookingStatus not Cancelled and not CheckedOut) are excluded from availability count. BR-UC04-04: Rooms in Maintenance status are excluded from availability. BR-UC04-05: Availability is calculated at the RoomCategory level by counting total rooms minus booked rooms with overlapping dates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Related Use Cases                                                 | UC06 View Room Details, UC22 Book Room                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Related Components                                                | **Controllers:** HomeController (SearchRooms, CheckRoomAvailability, RoomDetails) **Services:** IHomeService / HomeService (SearchAvailableCategoriesAsync, CheckAvailabilityAsync, GetRoomCategoriesForHomeAsync, GetRoomDetailsAsync) **Entities / Tables:** RoomCategories, Rooms, Bookings, BookingDetails                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Priority                                                          | High                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Frequency of Use                                                  | High                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Entities Affected                                                 | RoomCategories, Rooms, Bookings, BookingDetails                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
