| Field                                                        | Specification                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------------------------------| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Use Case ID                                                  | UC22                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Use Case Name                                                | Book Room                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Feature / Module                                             | Room Reservation & Front Desk                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Primary Actor                                                | Customer                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Secondary Actors                                             | VNPay Payment Gateway                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Description                                                  | As a customer, I want to book rooms by selecting room categories and quantities so that I can reserve accommodation at the resort, optionally adding tour packages, and pay the deposit via VNPay to confirm the booking.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Preconditions                                                | 1\. Customer is logged in (Authorize Role = "Customer"). 2\. Customer has selected at least one room category with quantity > 0 from the Search Rooms page. 3\. Check-in and check-out dates are valid.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Postconditions                                               | 1\. Booking record is created with status PendingPayment. 2\. BookingDetail records are created for each room (one per category × quantity). 3\. BookingGuest record is created for the primary guest. 4\. If tour add-ons are selected, TourBooking records are created with status Pending. 5\. Customer is redirected to VNPay to pay the deposit (40% of total).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Input Data                                                   | List of RoomSelections (CategoryId, Quantity), check-in date, check-out date, number of adults, number of children, primary guest name, primary guest date of birth, primary guest gender, primary guest passport number, primary guest nationality, PDPD consent checkbox, optional tour selections (ScheduleId, Participants)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Output Data                                                  | Booking record (BookingId), VNPay redirect URL for deposit payment                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Normal Sequence / Flow                                       | 1\. Customer is redirected to BookingsController.Create (GET) with RoomSelections, checkIn, checkOut, adults, children parameters. 2\. System validates at least one room selection has Quantity > 0; loads RoomCategory details and upcoming TourSchedules. 3\. System displays the booking form with room summary, guest information fields (primary guest), PDPD consent, and optional tour add-ons. 4\. Customer fills in primary guest information, agrees to PDPD, optionally selects tours, and submits the form. 5\. System validates: adults ≤ sum of (MaxAdults × Quantity per category), children ≤ sum of (MaxChildren × Quantity per category), PDPD consent checked, primary guest age ≥ 18. 6\. System calls BookingService.CreateBookingAsync which: validates dates (CheckOut > CheckIn, CheckIn ≥ today); checks room availability per category (total non-maintenance rooms minus overlapping non-cancelled/non-checked-out bookings ≥ requested quantity); calculates TotalAmount = Σ(PricePerNight × nights × quantity); creates Booking with status PendingPayment and BookingDetails (one per room). 7\. If tour add-ons selected: creates TourBooking records, validates available slots, adds tour costs to TotalAmount. 8\. DepositAmount = TotalAmount × 40%. 9\. System saves BookingGuest (primary guest) via BookingGuestService.SaveBookingGuestsAsync. 10\. System creates VNPay payment URL via VnPayService.CreatePaymentUrl and redirects customer to VNPay. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Room no longer available    | At step 6, if available rooms for a category < requested quantity (ErrorCode BKG-002): 1\. System returns error message. 2\. Customer is returned to the booking form.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Invalid booking information | At step 5, if ModelState is invalid (adults exceed capacity, PDPD not checked, age < 18, etc.): 1\. System redisplays the booking form with validation error messages. 2\. Customer corrects the information and submits again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Invalid dates               | At step 6, if CheckOut ≤ CheckIn or CheckIn < today (ErrorCode BKG-001): 1\. System displays "Ngày Check-in phải trước Check-out và lớn hơn hoặc bằng hôm nay."                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A4 – Tour slot insufficient      | At step 7, if requested tour participants exceed available slots (ErrorCode BKG-003): 1\. System displays tour capacity error message.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Exception Flow E1 – Booking creation error                   | If DbUpdateConcurrencyException occurs: 1\. System returns error code BKG-002 ("Room not available").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Business Rules                                               | BR-UC22-01: Double-booking prevention: checks overlapping BookingDetails by category for active bookings (not Cancelled, not CheckedOut). BR-UC22-02: Check-in date must be ≥ today and before check-out date. BR-UC22-03: Adults must not exceed sum of MaxAdults per category × quantity. BR-UC22-04: Children must not exceed sum of MaxChildren per category × quantity. BR-UC22-05: Primary guest must be ≥ 18 years old. BR-UC22-06: PDPD (Personal Data Protection Declaration) consent is mandatory. BR-UC22-07: Deposit is 40% of TotalAmount (room + tour if any). BR-UC22-08: Booking initial status is PendingPayment until deposit is paid via VNPay.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Related Use Cases                                            | UC04 Search Available Rooms, UC23 Make Deposit Payment, UC26 Book Tour (add-on), UC27 View Personal Invoice, UC28 View Booking History                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Related Components                                           | **Controllers:** BookingsController (Create GET/POST, Details, MyBookings, Cancel, PreCheckin, ViewInvoice) **Services:** IBookingService / BookingService (CreateBookingAsync, CancelBookingAsync), IBookingGuestService / BookingGuestService (SaveBookingGuestsAsync), IVnPayService / VnPayService (CreatePaymentUrl) **Entities / Tables:** Bookings, BookingDetails, BookingGuests, RoomCategories, Rooms, TourBookings, TourSchedules, Tours, Users                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Priority                                                     | Critical                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Frequency of Use                                             | High                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Entities Affected                                            | Bookings, BookingDetails, BookingGuests, RoomCategories, TourBookings, TourSchedules                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

#### ***2.2.3 UC23 – Make Deposit Payment***

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC23 |
| Use Case Name | Make Deposit Payment |
| Feature / Module | Room Reservation & Front Desk |
| Primary Actor | Customer |
| Secondary Actors | VNPay Payment Gateway, Email Service |
| Description | As a customer, I want to pay the room deposit via VNPay so that my room booking can be confirmed (status changed from PendingPayment to Reserved). |
| Preconditions | 1\. Customer has been redirected to VNPay after booking creation. 2\. Booking record exists with status PendingPayment. 3\. VNPay payment gateway is available. |
| Postconditions | 1\. VNPay payment is processed successfully. 2\. Booking status is updated to Reserved. 3\. Associated TourBookings (if any) are updated from Pending to Confirmed. 4\. Booking confirmation email is sent to the customer with booking details and tour QR codes (if applicable). 5\. Customer is redirected to MyBookings page. |
| Input Data | VNPay callback query parameters (transaction result, order ID, response code, amount) |
| Output Data | Payment result message, updated booking status, confirmation email with QR codes for tour tickets |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. After VNPay payment, customer is redirected to PaymentController.VNPayReturn. 2\. System calls VnPayService.ExecutePayment to parse and validate the VNPay callback. 3\. If payment is successful (response.Success = true): a. System loads the Booking (including User, BookingDetails, TourBookings with Schedules and Tours). b. System verifies booking status is PendingPayment. c. System updates BookingStatus to Reserved. d. System updates all associated TourBookings from Pending to Confirmed. e. System saves changes to database. f. System sends booking confirmation email (using BookingSuccess.html template) including: CustomerName, BookingId, RoomName, CheckInDate (14:00), CheckOutDate (12:00), TotalAmount, DepositAmount, and tour QR code tickets if applicable. g. System sets TempData success message. 4\. Customer is redirected to BookingsController.MyBookings. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Payment failed | At step 3, if VNPay response indicates failure: 1\. System sets TempData error message with VNPay response code. 2\. Booking remains in PendingPayment status. 3\. Customer is redirected to MyBookings. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – VNPay IPN callback | VNPay server calls PaymentController.VNPayIPN: 1\. System validates checksum (returns RspCode 97 if invalid). 2\. System finds booking by OrderId (returns RspCode 01 if not found). 3\. System checks idempotency: if booking is not PendingPayment, returns RspCode 02 ("Order already confirmed"). 4\. If payment successful: updates BookingStatus to Reserved, updates TourBookings to Confirmed, saves changes, returns RspCode 00. 5\. Otherwise returns RspCode 99. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Booking already confirmed | At step 3b, if booking status is not PendingPayment (already Reserved): 1\. System skips status update (idempotent). 2\. Customer is redirected to MyBookings with success message. |
| Exception Flow E1 – Payment callback error | If system cannot parse VNPay response or booking not found: 1\. Booking remains in PendingPayment status. 2\. System displays error message. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC23-01: VNPay is the only supported online payment gateway for deposit. BR-UC23-02: Deposit amount = 40% of TotalAmount (set during booking creation). BR-UC23-03: Only bookings with PendingPayment status can be confirmed via payment. BR-UC23-04: TourBookings linked to the booking are auto-confirmed when deposit is paid. BR-UC23-05: Confirmation email includes QR codes generated via external QR API for each tour ticket. BR-UC23-06: Both VNPayReturn (client redirect) and VNPayIPN (server callback) endpoints handle payment confirmation for reliability. |
| Related Use Cases | UC22 Book Room, UC26 Book Tour |
| Related Components | **Controllers:** PaymentController (VNPayReturn, VNPayIPN) **Services:** IVnPayService / VnPayService (CreatePaymentUrl, ExecutePayment), IEmailSender (SendEmailAsync) **Entities / Tables:** Bookings, TourBookings, Users |
| Priority | Critical |
| Frequency of Use | High |
| Entities Affected | Bookings, TourBookings |

#### ***2.2.4 UC51 – Perform Customer Check-in***

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC51 |
| Use Case Name | Perform Customer Check-in |
| Feature / Module | Room Reservation & Front Desk |
| Primary Actor | Receptionist |
| Secondary Actors | Customer, Email Service |
| Description | As a receptionist, I want to check guests in by assigning physical rooms, verifying guest identity, and recording stay registration information. The system supports a two-phase process: "Arrival Only" (mark guest as arrived without assigning rooms) and "Full Check-in" (assign physical rooms and mark as checked-in). |
| Preconditions | 1\. Receptionist is logged in (Role: Receptionist or Admin). 2\. Booking exists with status Reserved, PendingPayment, or Arrived. 3\. Guest identity information (BookingGuests) is available or entered at front desk. |
| Postconditions | **Arrival Only:** 1\. BookingStatus updated to Arrived. 2\. ActualCheckInTime recorded. 3\. GuestFolio created if not exists. **Full Check-in:** 1\. Physical rooms assigned to BookingDetails (RoomId set). 2\. Room status updated to Occupied. 3\. BookingStatus updated to CheckedIn. 4\. ActualCheckInTime and RoomHandoverTime recorded. 5\. GuestFolio created with early check-in surcharge if applicable. 6\. BookingGuests saved via BookingGuestService. 7\. Check-in email sent to customer. |
| Input Data | BookingId, IsArrivalOnly flag, guest information (FullName, DateOfBirth, Gender, PassportNumber, Nationality, IsPrimaryBooker) for each guest, RoomAssignments (BookingDetailId → SelectedRoomId) when doing full check-in |
| Output Data | Updated booking status, assigned rooms, updated room statuses, GuestFolio record, check-in result message |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Receptionist opens ReceptionController.Index, searches/filters bookings by keyword, status, or date. 2\. System displays booking list with booking details, room categories, guest info. 3\. Receptionist clicks Check-in for a booking → ReceptionController.CheckIn (GET). 4\. System calls ReceptionService.PrepareCheckInViewModelAsync: loads booking, existing BookingGuests, generates RoomAssignments with available rooms per BookingDetail (via GetAvailableRoomsForCategoryAsync). 5\. System displays check-in form with: guest registration fields (prefilled if pre-checkin was done), room assignment dropdowns per BookingDetail. 6\. Receptionist fills/verifies guest information, selects physical rooms for each BookingDetail, chooses "Arrival Only" or "Full Check-in". 7\. Receptionist submits → ReceptionController.CheckIn (POST). 8\. System validates: primary guest must have FullName, DateOfBirth, PassportNumber, Nationality; if not ArrivalOnly, each BookingDetail must have a selected room; no duplicate room assignments. 9\. System calls ReceptionService.ProcessCheckInAsync which: validates booking status (must be Reserved, PendingPayment, or Arrived); **If ArrivalOnly:** sets ActualCheckInTime, updates status to Arrived, creates GuestFolio; **If Full Check-in:** assigns rooms (detail.RoomId = selectedRoomId), sets room status to Occupied, sets ActualCheckInTime and RoomHandoverTime, updates status to CheckedIn, creates GuestFolio, calculates early check-in surcharge if RoomHandoverTime is before 14:00 on CheckInDate (before 6h: 100%, 6-9h: 50%, 9-11h: 30%, 11-14h: free), adds surcharge as FolioTransaction(RoomCharge). 10\. System saves BookingGuests. 11\. System sends check-in email (CheckInSuccess.html template) with room number, check-in time, check-out date, guest count. 12\. Receptionist is redirected to booking list with success message. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Booking not found | System returns NotFound. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Invalid booking status | If booking is not Reserved/PendingPayment/Arrived: 1\. System sets TempData error message. 2\. Receptionist is redirected to booking list. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Room already occupied or in maintenance | During room assignment validation: 1\. System checks room status (rejects Maintenance and Occupied rooms). 2\. System checks for overlapping bookings on the same physical room. 3\. If conflict found, returns error message with the room number. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A4 – Room category mismatch | If assigned room's CategoryId doesn't match BookingDetail's RoomCategoryId: 1\. System rejects with error "Phòng [number] không thuộc loại phòng yêu cầu." |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A5 – Walk-in Booking | Receptionist creates a new booking at the front desk via ReceptionController.WalkInBooking: 1\. Enters customer phone, name, email, check-in/out dates, selects physical rooms directly. 2\. System auto-creates or finds Customer account by phone number. 3\. If InstantCheckIn: assigns rooms, sets status to CheckedIn, creates GuestFolio, saves guests, sends email. 4\. If not InstantCheckIn: creates booking with status Reserved. 5\. Minimum deposit = 40% of total room charges. |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error | ProcessCheckInAsync catches exceptions, returns error message, booking/room status unchanged. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC51-01: Only bookings with status Reserved, PendingPayment, or Arrived can be checked in. BR-UC51-02: A booking cannot be checked in if already CheckedIn, CheckedOut, or Cancelled. BR-UC51-03: Each BookingDetail must be assigned a unique physical room (no duplicate assignments). BR-UC51-04: Assigned room must match the BookingDetail's RoomCategoryId. BR-UC51-05: Rooms in Maintenance or Occupied status cannot be assigned. BR-UC51-06: Room status becomes Occupied after full check-in. BR-UC51-07: Early check-in surcharge applies if RoomHandoverTime is before 14:00 on the check-in date (tiered: 100%/50%/30%/free). BR-UC51-08: Walk-in booking requires minimum 40% deposit. BR-UC51-09: Walk-in booking auto-creates Customer account with default password "123456" if phone number not found. |
| Related Use Cases | UC49 Manage Room Bookings, UC52 Submit Stay Registration (Pre Check-in), UC56 Update Room Operational Status |
| Related Components | **Controllers:** ReceptionController (Index, CheckIn GET/POST, WalkInBooking GET/POST, GetAvailableRoomsJson, ExportManifest) **Services:** IReceptionService / ReceptionService (GetBookingsAsync, GetBookingByIdAsync, PrepareCheckInViewModelAsync, ProcessCheckInAsync, GetAvailableRoomsForCategoryAsync, GetAvailableRoomsForDatesAsync, ProcessWalkInBookingAsync), IBookingGuestService / BookingGuestService (SaveBookingGuestsAsync, GetBookingGuestsForCheckInAsync), IEmailSender (SendEmailAsync) **Entities / Tables:** Bookings, BookingDetails, BookingGuests, Rooms, RoomCategories, GuestFolios, FolioTransactions, Users |
| Priority | Critical |
| Frequency of Use | High |
| Entities Affected | Bookings, BookingDetails, BookingGuests, Rooms, GuestFolios, FolioTransactions, Users |

#### ***2.2.5 UC52 – Submit Stay Registration (Pre Check-in)***

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC52 |
| Use Case Name | Submit Stay Registration (Pre Check-in) |
| Feature / Module | Room Reservation & Front Desk |
| Primary Actor | Customer / Receptionist |
| Secondary Actors | Booking Guest |
| Description | As a customer or receptionist, I want to submit guest stay registration (temporary residence) information before or during check-in, so that the resort complies with Vietnamese temporary residence regulations (Law on Residence 2020) and PDPD requirements. The customer can fill out the form online via a shareable token-based link, or the receptionist can complete it at the front desk. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Booking exists in the system. 2\. For customer access: customer is logged in as booking owner, OR has a valid pre-checkin token. 3\. For receptionist access: check-in form includes guest registration section. |
| Postconditions | 1\. BookingGuest records are saved for primary booker and companion guests. 2\. PDPD consent status is recorded on the booking. 3\. Guest data is available for temporary residence report export (CSV via ReceptionController.ExportManifest). |
| Input Data | BookingId, PDPD consent checkbox, for each guest: FullName, DateOfBirth, Gender (Male/Female/Other), PassportNumber, Nationality, IsPrimaryBooker flag |
| Output Data | Saved BookingGuest records, success/error message, shareable pre-checkin link |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Customer accesses BookingsController.PreCheckin (GET) with booking ID. 2\. System verifies access: checks if user is booking owner; if not, validates the token parameter (SHA256 hash of BookingId + CheckInDate.Ticks + secret key). 3\. System loads existing BookingGuests, pre-fills guest slots up to total capacity (NumberOfAdults + NumberOfChildren). 4\. System displays the pre-checkin form with: booking summary, PDPD consent, guest registration fields for each slot, a shareable link for other guests to fill their info. 5\. Customer fills in guest information and checks PDPD consent. 6\. Customer submits → BookingsController.PreCheckin (POST). 7\. System validates: PDPD must be checked; primary booker must have FullName, DateOfBirth, Gender, PassportNumber, Nationality; companion guests with any field filled must have FullName, DateOfBirth, Gender, Nationality. 8\. System calls BookingGuestService.SaveBookingGuestsAsync to save valid guest records. 9\. System displays success message; if owner, redirects to MyBookings; if token user, redirects to PreCheckinSuccess page. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Missing required information | At step 7, if required fields missing: 1\. System displays per-field validation errors. 2\. Customer corrects and resubmits. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – PDPD consent not checked | At step 7: 1\. System displays error "Bạn phải đồng ý với điều khoản bảo vệ dữ liệu cá nhân (PDPD) để tiếp tục." |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – Token-based guest access | If user is not logged in or not the booking owner: 1\. System validates token. 2\. If token invalid, returns Forbid. 3\. If no token and not logged in, redirects to Login with returnUrl. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A4 – Export temporary residence report | Receptionist accesses ReceptionController.ExportManifest: 1\. System generates CSV file (UTF-8 with BOM) containing guest list: STT, FullName, DateOfBirth, Gender, PassportNumber, Nationality, Role, Duration. 2\. Receptionist can also view print-friendly manifest via PrintManifest view. |
| Exception Flow E1 – Save error | If BookingGuestService throws exception: 1\. System displays error message. 2\. Guest data is not saved. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC52-01: PDPD (Personal Data Protection Declaration) consent is mandatory. BR-UC52-02: Primary booker must provide: FullName, DateOfBirth, Gender, PassportNumber, Nationality. BR-UC52-03: Companion guests are optional but if any field is filled, FullName, DateOfBirth, Gender, and Nationality are required. BR-UC52-04: Pre-checkin token is generated using SHA256(BookingId + CheckInDate.Ticks + secret key) for security. BR-UC52-05: Guest registration data must be available for temporary residence report export (CSV format). BR-UC52-06: The shareable link allows non-account holders to submit their guest information. |
| Related Use Cases | UC17 Update CCCD/Passport, UC51 Perform Customer Check-in, UC58 Export Temporary Residence Report |
| Related Components | **Controllers:** BookingsController (PreCheckin GET/POST, PreCheckinSuccess), ReceptionController (ExportManifest) **Services:** IBookingGuestService / BookingGuestService (SaveBookingGuestsAsync, GetBookingGuestsForCheckInAsync), IReceptionService / ReceptionService (GetManifestDataAsync) **Entities / Tables:** Bookings, BookingGuests |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | High |
| Entities Affected | Bookings, BookingGuests |

#### ***2.2.6 UC53 – Perform Customer Check-out***

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC53 |
| Use Case Name | Perform Customer Check-out |
| Feature / Module | Room Reservation & Front Desk |
| Primary Actor | Receptionist |
| Secondary Actors | Customer, Email Service |
| Description | As a receptionist, I want to check guests out by reviewing all charges (room, food, tour), calculating late check-out surcharges, processing final payment, generating a consolidated invoice, and updating room status to NeedsCleaning. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Receptionist is logged in (Role: Receptionist or Admin). 2\. Booking exists with status CheckedIn. 3\. GuestFolio exists or is auto-created for the booking. |
| Postconditions | 1\. GuestFolio status updated to Paid. 2\. Invoice record created with status Paid. 3\. Payment record created for balance due (if > 0). 4\. Booking status updated to CheckedOut. 5\. ActualCheckOutTime recorded. 6\. All assigned rooms' status updated to NeedsCleaning. 7\. Check-out email sent to customer. |
| Input Data | BookingId, FolioId, selected payment method (Cash, CreditCard, DebitCard, VNPay, MoMo, BankTransfer), late check-out surcharge amount, balance due amount |
| Output Data | Checked-out booking, paid invoice (InvoiceNumber format: INV-yyyyMMdd-FolioId), closed folio, updated room statuses, checkout result message, printed invoice |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Receptionist clicks Check-out for a checked-in booking → ReceptionController.CheckOut (GET). 2\. System calls ReceptionService.PrepareCheckOutViewModelAsync which: a. Loads booking with user, booking details, rooms. b. Verifies booking status is CheckedIn. c. Loads or creates GuestFolio with FolioTransactions. d. Compensates for folios that missed base room charge during check-in (adds TotalAmount as display charge). e. Calculates late check-out surcharge if current time > 12:00 on CheckOutDate: 12:00-15:00 = 30% of one night's room price; 15:00-18:00 = 50%; after 18:00 = 100%. f. Calculates BalanceDue = max(0, TotalAmount - DepositAmount). g. Maps FolioTransactions into display DTOs with resolved names (room charge, food charge by type, tour charge, early/late surcharges). 3\. System displays checkout page with: guest name, check-in/out dates, room numbers, room charges, food charges, tour charges, late surcharge, total amount, deposit paid, balance due, folio transaction list, payment method selector. 4\. Receptionist reviews charges with customer, selects payment method (required if balance due > 0). 5\. Receptionist confirms check-out → ReceptionController.CheckOut (POST). 6\. System calls ReceptionService.ProcessCheckOutAsync which: a. Validates booking status is CheckedIn and folio exists. b. Validates payment method is selected if balance due > 0. c. Adds late surcharge FolioTransaction if applicable. d. Compensates base room charge in folio if missing. e. Updates folio status to Paid. f. Creates Invoice (TotalAmount, FinalAmount, status Paid, issued now). g. Creates Payment record (Amount = BalanceDue, method, status Success). h. Updates booking status to CheckedOut, sets ActualCheckOutTime. i. Updates all assigned rooms' status to NeedsCleaning. j. Saves all changes to database. k. Sends check-out email (CheckOutSuccess.html template). 7\. Receptionist is redirected to PrintInvoice page. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Balance due > 0, no payment method selected | At step 6a: 1\. System returns error "Vui lòng chọn phương thức thanh toán." 2\. Checkout form is redisplayed. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Booking not CheckedIn | At step 2b: 1\. System returns null, redirects with error "Không ở trạng thái có thể Check-out." |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – View/Print Invoice | After checkout, receptionist or customer can view invoice via: ReceptionController.PrintInvoice or BookingsController.ViewInvoice. System calls ReceptionService.PrepareInvoiceViewModelAsync which loads booking, folio, invoice, payments, and transactions. |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error | ProcessCheckOutAsync returns error message; booking/room/folio status unchanged. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC53-01: Only CheckedIn bookings can be checked out. BR-UC53-02: Late check-out surcharge tiers (based on current time vs 12:00 on checkout date): 12-15h = 30%, 15-18h = 50%, ≥18h = 100% of one night's room price. BR-UC53-03: Payment method is required if balance due > 0. BR-UC53-04: Supported payment methods: Cash, CreditCard, DebitCard, VNPay, MoMo, BankTransfer. BR-UC53-05: Invoice number format: INV-yyyyMMdd-FolioId. BR-UC53-06: Room status must become NeedsCleaning after checkout. BR-UC53-07: Folio base room charge compensation: if the folio doesn't include the booking's TotalAmount as a room charge, it is added automatically during checkout. BR-UC53-08: FolioTransaction types include: RoomCharge (base room + early/late surcharges), FoodCharge (room service / table service), TourCharge. |
| Related Use Cases | UC51 Perform Customer Check-in, UC56 Update Room Operational Status |
| Related Components | **Controllers:** ReceptionController (CheckOut GET/POST, PrintInvoice), BookingsController (ViewInvoice) **Services:** IReceptionService / ReceptionService (PrepareCheckOutViewModelAsync, ProcessCheckOutAsync, PrepareInvoiceViewModelAsync), IEmailSender (SendEmailAsync) **Entities / Tables:** Bookings, BookingDetails, Rooms, GuestFolios, FolioTransactions, Invoices, Payments |
| Priority | Critical |
| Frequency of Use | High |
| Entities Affected | Bookings, BookingDetails, Rooms, GuestFolios, FolioTransactions, Invoices, Payments |

#### ***2.2.7 UC56 – Update Room Operational Status***

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC56 |
| Use Case Name | Update Room Operational Status |
| Feature / Module | Room Reservation & Front Desk |
| Primary Actor | Business Manager / Receptionist / Housekeeping Staff |
| Secondary Actors | None |
| Description | As a business manager, receptionist, or housekeeping staff, I want to view and update room operational statuses so that room availability and operational conditions remain accurate for booking, check-in, housekeeping, and maintenance workflows. |
| Preconditions | 1\. User is logged in with role Business Manager, Receptionist, or Housekeeping Staff. 2\. Room exists in the system. |
| Postconditions | 1\. Room operational status is updated. 2\. Updated status is reflected in room search, check-in, and housekeeping workflows. |
| Input Data | Room ID, new room status (Available, Occupied, Maintenance, NeedsCleaning, CleaningInProgress), room number, category, floor number |
| Output Data | Updated room record, success redirect to room list |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. User opens RoomsController.Index which displays a paginated room list (10 per page) with search, status filter, and category filter. 2\. User selects a room to edit → RoomsController.Edit (GET). 3\. System loads room data and available categories, displays edit form. 4\. User selects new status from: Available, Occupied, Maintenance, NeedsCleaning, CleaningInProgress. 5\. User submits → RoomsController.Edit (POST). 6\. System validates ModelState, updates room record via RoomService.UpdateRoomAsync. 7\. User is redirected to room list. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Create new room (Business Manager only) | 1\. Business Manager opens RoomsController.Create. 2\. Enters room number, category, floor number. 3\. System creates room with default status Available. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Delete room (Business Manager only) | 1\. Business Manager opens RoomsController.Delete, confirms deletion. 2\. System deletes room via RoomService.DeleteRoomAsync. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A3 – View room details | 1\. User opens RoomsController.Details to view room information. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A4 – Automatic status changes | Room status is also changed automatically by other processes: a. Check-in (UC51): Room → Occupied. b. Check-out (UC53): Room → NeedsCleaning. c. Housekeeping workflows may change NeedsCleaning → CleaningInProgress → Available. |
| Exception Flow E1 – Room not found | If room ID doesn't exist: System returns NotFound. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC56-01: Room status enum values: Available, Occupied, Maintenance, NeedsCleaning, CleaningInProgress. BR-UC56-02: Only Business Manager can create or delete rooms. BR-UC56-03: All three roles (Business Manager, Receptionist, Housekeeping Staff) can view and edit rooms. BR-UC56-04: Rooms in Maintenance status are excluded from availability searches and cannot be assigned during check-in. BR-UC56-05: Rooms with status Occupied cannot be assigned to another booking. BR-UC56-06: After checkout, rooms are automatically set to NeedsCleaning. |
| Related Use Cases | UC51 Perform Customer Check-in, UC53 Perform Customer Check-out, UC60 View Room Cleaning Tasks |
| Related Components | **Controllers:** RoomsController (Index, Details, Create GET/POST, Edit GET/POST, Delete GET/POST) **Services:** IRoomService / RoomService (GetAllRoomsAsync, GetRoomByIdAsync, AddRoomAsync, UpdateRoomAsync, DeleteRoomAsync), IRoomCategoryService / RoomCategoryService (GetAllAsync) **Entities / Tables:** Rooms, RoomCategories |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | High |
| Entities Affected | Rooms, RoomCategories |


### **2.3 Restaurant POS & Room Service** 

#### ***2.3.1*** **UC24 – Reserve Restaurant Table**

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC24 |
| Use Case Name | Reserve Restaurant Table |

| Primary Actor | Customer |
| Secondary Actors | Receptionist |
| Description | As a customer, I want to reserve a restaurant table so that I can dine at the resort restaurant at my preferred time. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Customer is logged in. 2\. Restaurant table information exists in the system. 3\. Restaurant is operating during the requested reservation time. |
| Postconditions | 1\. Restaurant reservation is created. 2\. Reserved table is allocated for the selected time slot. 3\. Reservation information is available to restaurant staff. |
| Input Data | Customer ID, reservation date, reservation time, number of guests, special requests |
| Output Data | Restaurant reservation record, reservation code, reservation status |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Customer opens Restaurant Reservation page. 2\. System displays available reservation information. 3\. Customer enters reservation details. 4\. Customer submits reservation request. 5\. System checks table availability. 6\. System creates reservation. 7\. System displays reservation confirmation. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – No table available | At step 5, if no suitable table is available: 1\. System rejects the reservation request. 2\. System displays unavailable table message. 3\. Customer may select another time slot. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Invalid reservation information | System displays validation messages and requests correction. |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error | Reservation cannot be created and system displays an error message. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC24-01: Reservation time must be within restaurant operating hours. BR-UC24-02: Guest count must not exceed table capacity. BR-UC24-03: Table cannot be double-booked for the same time slot. |
| Related Use Cases | UC09 View Restaurant Menu |
| Related Components | Controllers: RestaurantReservationController Services: RestaurantReservationService, TableAvailabilityService Repositories / DAO: RestaurantReservationRepository, TableRepository Entities / Tables: RestaurantReservations, Tables |

| Frequency of Use | Medium |
| Entities Affected | RestaurantReservations, Tables |

System validates the order information. 6\. System creates the food order. 7\. System sends the order to restaurant operations. 8\. System displays order confirmation. |

| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Invalid status transition | System rejects the update and displays a warning message. |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error | System cannot save the status update and displays an error message. |

System validates room occupancy. 6\. System posts the charge to the guest folio. 7\. System displays successful charge confirmation. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Room not occupied | System rejects the charge request and displays warning message. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Invalid room information | System requests room information correction. The food order has already been charged:  1\. System detects the order was previously charged to the room.  2\. System displays an error message "Order already charged to room" and prevents the action.  |
| Exception Flow E1 – Posting error | Charge is not posted and system displays an error message. |

BR-UC69-03: Food charge must appear in consolidated billing. BR-UC69-04: A completed food order can only be charged to a room ONCE. The system must prevent duplicate charges for the same order.   |

### **2.4 Tour Booking & Feedback System**

#### ***2.4.1*** **UC26 – Book Tour** 

| Field | Specification |
| ----- | ----- |
| Use Case ID | UC26 |
| Use Case Name | Book Tour |
| Feature / Module | Tour Booking & Feedback System |
| Primary Actor | Customer |
| Secondary Actors | Tour Guide |
| Description | As a customer, I want to book a tour so that I can participate in resort activities during my stay. |
| Preconditions | 1\. Customer is logged in. 2\. Tour information and tour schedule exist in the system. 3\. Selected tour schedule is active and available. |
| Postconditions | 1\. Tour booking is created. 2\. Number of available tour slots is updated. 3\. Tour booking can be included in the guest’s consolidated invoice. |
| Input Data | Customer ID, tour schedule ID, number of participants, booking note |
| Output Data | Tour booking record, tour booking status, booking confirmation message |
| Normal Sequence / Flow | 1\. Customer opens Tour List or Tour Detail page. 2\. System displays tour information and available schedules. 3\. Customer selects a tour schedule and number of participants. 4\. Customer submits the tour booking request. 5\. System validates tour availability and participant count. 6\. System creates the tour booking. 7\. System displays tour booking confirmation. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A1 – Tour schedule unavailable | At step 5, if the selected schedule is unavailable: 1\. System rejects the booking request. 2\. System displays unavailable schedule message. 3\. Customer may select another schedule. |
| Alternative Sequence / Flow A2 – Participant count exceeds available slots | At step 5, if participant count exceeds available slots: 1\. System rejects the booking request. 2\. System displays available slot information. 3\. Customer may reduce participant count or select another schedule. |
| Exception Flow E1 – System error | If system error occurs during booking: 1\. System does not create the tour booking. 2\. System displays a general error message. 3\. System records the error. |
| Business Rules | BR-UC26-01: Customer can only book active tour schedules. BR-UC26-02: Number of participants must be greater than zero. BR-UC26-03: Number of participants must not exceed available capacity. BR-UC26-04: Tour booking must be linked to the customer or active booking/folio if applicable. BR-UC26-05: Tour charges must be available for consolidated billing. |
| Related Use Cases | UC12 View Tour Details UC54 Generate Consolidated Invoice UC29 Submit Reviews |
| Related Components | Controllers: TourBookingController, TourController Services: TourBookingService, TourScheduleService Repositories / DAO: TourBookingRepository, TourScheduleRepository, TourRepository Entities / Tables: TourBookings, TourSchedules, Tours, Users |
| Priority | High |
| Frequency of Use | Medium |
| Entities Affected | TourBookings, TourSchedules, Tours, Users |

Customer enters star rating and written review. 5\. Customer submits the review. 6\. System validates review information. 7\. System saves the review.8. System displays review submission result. |

| Exception Flow E1 – System error | If system error occurs during review submission: 1\. System does not save the review. 2\. System displays a general error message. 3\. System records the error. |

### **3.3 System Administration** {#3.3-system-administration}

#### ***3.3.1 Master Data*** {#3.3.1-master-data}

##### **3.3.1.1 Setting List** {#3.3.1.1-setting-list}

 

This screen allows the Administrator to:

● View Setting List: view list of current master data.

● Filter Setting List: filter master data by data types, statuses

● Search Settings: enter keyword(s) to search master data by their names or values

● Sort Setting List: sort master data list (ascending, descending) by clicking column headers

On the screen, s/he can also

● Activate/Deactivate Setting: change status of a specific inactive/active master data

● Choose to go to the Setting Details screens for adding new or updating an existing master data by clicking the New Setting or Edit link.

**Field Description**

| Field Name | Description |
| ----- | :---- |
| (1) | Initial values: all the active setting names with null or blank type Hover the mouse to show the field name: “Setting Type” |
| (2) | Initial values: All Statuses, Active, Inactive (default value “All Status”) Hover the mouse to show the field name: “Setting Status” |
| (3) | The change-status action is Activate or Deactivate depending on the current status of the relevant setting (Inactive or Active, respectively). |

 

##### **3.3.1.2 Setting Details** {#3.3.1.2-setting-details}

 

 

 

This screen allows the Administrator to:

● Add New Setting: add new master data.

● Update Setting Details: update details of a specific master data

**Field Description**

| Field Name | Description |
| :---- | :---- |
| Name | Data type: non-digit string, max length of 20 characters |
| Type | Initial data values: all active setting names (with null or blank type) |
| Value | Data type: any string, max length of 100 characters |
| Priority | Data type: a positive integer |
| Description | Data type: any string, max length of 200 characters |

## **5\. Requirement Appendix** {#5.-requirement-appendix}

*\[Provide business rules, common requirements, or other extra requirements information here\]*

### **5.1 Business Rules** {#5.1-business-rules}

*\[Provide common business rules that you must follow. The information can be provided in the table format as the sample below\]*

| ID | Rule Definition |
| ----- | ----- |
| BR-01 | Delivery time windows are 15 minutes, beginning on each quarter hour. |
| BR-02 | Deliveries must be completed between 10:00 A.M. and 2:00 P.M. local time, inclusive. |
| BR-03 | All meals in a single order must be delivered to the same location. |
| BR-04 | All meals in a single order must be paid for by using the same payment method. |
| BR-11 | If an order is to be delivered, the patron must pay by payroll deduction. |
| BR-12 | Order price is calculated as the sum of each food item price times the quantity of that food item ordered, plus applicable sales tax, plus a delivery charge if a meal is delivered outside the free delivery zone. |
| BR-24 | Only cafeteria employees who are designated as Menu Managers by the Cafeteria Manager can create, modify, or delete cafeteria menus. |
| BR-33 | Network transmissions that involve financial information or personally identifiable information require 256-bit encryption. |
| BR-86 | Only regular employees can register for payroll deduction for any company purchase. |
| BR-88 | An employee can register for payroll deduction payment of cafeteria meals if no more than 40 percent of his gross pay is currently being deducted for other reasons. |

 

### **5.2 System Messages** {#5.2-system-messages}

| \# | Message code | Message Type | Context | Content |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| 1 | MSG01 | In line | There is not any search result | *No search results.*   |
| 2 | MSG02 | In red, under the text box | Input-required fields are empty | *The \* field is required.* |
| 3 | MSG03 | Toast message | Updating asset(s) information successfully | *Update asset(s) successfully.* |
| 4 | MSG04 | Toast message | Adding new asset successfully | *Add asset successfully.* |
| 5 | MSG05 | Toast message | Confirming email of asset hand-over is sent successfully | *A confirmation email has been sent to {email\_address}.* |
